# tldr

tldr pages: short practical examples of each command, for every platform, Linux and Windows. Version 2.3, from <https://github.com/tldr-pages/tldr> at v2.3, commit 3b17800dbd75c5e19287e2f955c5886c446d2ec3.
License: CC-BY-4.0, by the tldr-pages contributors; see THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md. Each file is the official file, unchanged.

This index is long: search it for your words, never read it whole. Then read one section of one file.
